The candle finalization should be running in time like 10:00AM, 10:05AM, 10:10AM ETC, NOT LIKE 10:01AM, 10:02AM ETC. The time should be a multiple of 5

	public void start(String[] instruments) {
        if (isRunning) {
            System.out.println("⚠️ RealTimeCandleBuilder is already running");
            return;
        }

        System.out.println("🚀 Starting RealTimeCandleBuilder for " + instruments.length + " instruments");
        isRunning = true;

        // Clear any old data
        tickDataMap.clear();
        currentCandleMap.clear();

        // Schedule price fetch every 1 seconds
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> fetchAndProcessPrices(instruments), 0, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS); // Fetch every 1 seconds

        // Schedule candle finalization every minute (to check if 5-min period ended)
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::checkAndFinalizeCandles, 0, 60, TimeUnit.SECONDS); // Check every minute

        System.out.println("✅ RealTimeCandleBuilder started");
    }
